# Making: Blocks, Wiring and a Thinking Machine Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Part B of the Mind and Making spec: let Mimo make things, and build a working machine the owner can watch. T1 New blocks and crafts: 35 new blocks and 6 new items (paper, books and bookshelves, dyes, coloured wool and rugs, clay bricks and a kiln, stairs, slabs, glass panes, trapdoors, iron bars, flower pots, signs, barrels, composters and candles), each with a soft-pixel texture of our own; clay and sand among the shore gravel; Mimo makes what a project needs where it stands and gathers the clay, sand and sugar cane for it; a workshop (a crafting table, furnace, kiln and barrel under a slab roof) and a cozy home (windows, a bookshelf, a rug of its favourite colour, a pot, a candle, a sign and a composter) as goals. T2 Wiring: copper wire, levers, buttons, pressure plates, daylight sensors, repeaters, inverters, joiners, lamps, doors and bells, run by a deterministic server-side signal engine bounded to 256 powered cells a tick; the lesson "copper carries a spark" from an old manual in deep copper, the owner's teaching (Mind) or tinkering; the first circuits (a lamp on a lever, an automatic door, a night-light). T3 The thinking machine: a clock, a memory cell, a counter and Mimo's computer, a 4-bit counter driven by a daylight sensor that shows the game days Mimo has lived in binary on four lamps, with a bell that rings at dawn and a lever that shows or hides it; the HUD caption ("Pip's computer: day 13 = 1101") and a Workshop panel.

**Architecture:** Blocks go at the end of `shared/blocks.json`, so no id changes, with two new render shapes (`slab`, half high, and `flat`, a sixteenth high) that `blocks.ts` and the mesher draw at their height; `atlas.ts` gains 20 tile painters. Worldgen changes in one place, the same in both languages: a gravel shore cell is clay 1 time in 5 and sand 1 time in 5. Making is a set of modules that register into the hooks the brain already reads: `making.py` (what projects need, `craft_plan`, the `gather_materials` purpose; `storage.KEEPS_MORE`, `work.MORE_ORES`, and the new `work.MORE_STONE` and `building.MORE_BLOCKS`), `workshop.py` and `cozy.py` (structures, purposes and goals), `signals.py` (the engine, a `machine_signals` table, run last in the tick's transaction), `machines.py` (the `Machine` registry, designs from rule layouts, the `build_machine` purpose, the `flip` step, the first circuits goal, the viewer's `workshop` field), `tinker.py` (the four making lessons in `journal.LESSONS`, the old manual, the `tinker` purpose) and `computer.py` (the four machines of the thinking machine and its goal). The viewer draws lit parts as their own glowing blocks, holds doors open that a machine opens, and gets `workshop.ts` (the caption and the panel's rows) and `WorkshopPanel.tsx`.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.10+ (3.12 in Docker), FastAPI, SQLite, `unittest`; React 19, three.js 0.184, @react-three/fiber 9, Vite 8, TypeScript 6, Vitest 5.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-mind-and-making-design.md`, Part B: Making (T1, T2, T3, and its Viewer section). Part A (Mind) is planned separately; this plan does not need its code (resolution 14). It builds on L4a and L4b (landed) and Bond B1 (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-25-bond.md`, Tasks 1–6, landed with its fix rounds).

## Global Constraints

- Stay on branch `worthy/23_09_2026/survival_core`. Do not switch branches.
- Python must run on 3.10 locally and 3.12 in Docker. Every new Python module starts with `from __future__ import annotations`. No backslash inside an f-string's braces (3.10).
- Backend tests use `unittest` and call functions directly. Never import `backend.main` in a test (it needs `redis`, which is not installed locally).
- Tests never make real network or model calls. Making makes no model call of its own: its purposes and goals are offered to Jev in the Chooser's existing calls, like every other, and tests that run the brain use the fake Jev pattern (`test_survival_goal_choice.Recorder`) or no key. Nothing reads `random` or the clock in a test: every roll is `nature.roll` on the world seed and a cell (the old manual, tinkering).
- TypeScript has `erasableSyntaxOnly`, `noUnusedLocals` and `noUnusedParameters`. No constructor parameter properties, no enums, no namespaces.
- React runs in StrictMode. `eslint-plugin-react-hooks` 7 is on: no `Date.now()` or ref reads during render, never mutate props, state or hook arguments, no synchronous `setState` in an effect body.
- Vitest runs in the `node` environment and only picks up `src/**/*.test.ts`. Viewer logic that needs tests lives in `.ts` modules, not in components. No stylesheet changes: Tailwind classes inline, as the HUD has them.
- SQLite: WAL, `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` for writes, `busy_timeout`. Making adds one table, `machine_signals` (a row a machine, written only when its circuit changed), created by `create_world_tables` (`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`); a world only ever read (an archive) may lack it and every reader treats that as no signals. Making adds no column. Its brain bookkeeping lives in `state["brain"]` (`machines_tried`, `tinker`, `computer_told`) and the engine's turn in `state["signal_turn"]`. Read-only GETs never write: `/api/mimo`'s `workshop` field only reads.
- **No model call inside the tick.** The signal engine is plain arithmetic, run last in the tick's transaction, bounded to `MAX_CELLS` = 256 cells worked out per world per tick (resolution 8). Every new hook is crash-guarded and logged once (`once.log_once`): a machine's run, `observe_tinker`, `observe_computer`, `tend_comfort`, each entry of `making.NEEDS`, `work.MORE_ORES` and `work.MORE_STONE`. A crashing machine never stops the tick.
- **Worldgen parity.** `backend/services/worldgen.py` and `frontend/src/engine/worldgen.ts` change together, in Task 2 only, with the same constant and channel. Heights never change and the legacy clearing keeps every block (resolution 3). Regenerate the fixture after Task 2 (`python3 -m backend.scripts.worldgen_fixture`).
- New blocks go at the end of `shared/blocks.json`: T1's after L3's `creature_sprout`, T2's after T1's `candle`. Items that are not blocks: `paper`, `book`, `dye_orange`, `dye_pink`, `dye_yellow`, `tallow`.
- Values from the spec (Making): about 30 blocks and items with textures (35 blocks and 6 items); coloured wool from flower dyes and a favourite colour trait; clay bricks and a kiln; stairs and slabs; glass panes; trapdoors; copper wire; iron bars; flower pots; signs; barrels; a composter that speeds crops; candles from tallow, from sheep; a workshop (a crafting table, furnace, kiln and barrel under one roof) as a goal; wire carrying a signal up to 15 blocks, weakening like redstone; sources lever, button (a 1-second pulse), pressure plate, daylight sensor; logic repeater (delay and boost), inverter (NOT), joiner (OR/AND by arrangement); outputs lamp, door (powered opens), bell (a sound event); the engine server-side, updating only what changed, breadth first, bounded to 256 powered cells per world per tick; the lesson "copper carries a spark" three ways in (the owner, an old manual when digging deep copper until L5, tinkering); a lesson for each larger machine (clock, latch, adder); the machines a lamp on a lever, an automatic door, a night-light, a clock, a memory cell (two inverters), a counter (4 latches and a clock) and Mimo's computer (a 4-bit counter with a lamp display counting the game days Mimo has lived, in binary, a lever to show and hide it, a bell at dawn, and "I built a machine that remembers how long I've been alive!"); each machine a structure with a blueprint and an engine test; the viewer draws every new block, wire flat, dim when off and bright when on, lamps that glow when lit, a HUD caption ("Mimo's computer: day 13 = 1101") and a "Workshop" panel of the machines built.
- Never run `docker` or `docker compose` in the numbered code tasks. Task 14's manual check is the controller's, on the demo stack (`mimo-l3demo-api` on :8011 and `mimo-l3demo-worker`, volume `mimo_l3demo`, the viewer on :3000); never touch, mount or migrate the owner's real volume `pets_mimo_data` or the real stack (`pets-api-1`, `pets-mimo-worker-1`). Never print `TYPESAFE_API_KEY`, `MIMO_MODEL_API_KEY`, `OPENAI_API_KEY` or any other key.
- `docs/` is in `.gitignore`. Add files under `docs/` with `git add -f`.
- End every commit message with a blank line and `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>` (the second `-m` in each commit command does this).

## Commands

- Backend tests, from the repo root: `python3 -m unittest discover -s backend/tests` (1,274 pass at `254c452`, 2 skipped; 1,357 after Task 14)
- One backend test file: `python3 -m unittest discover -s backend/tests -p "test_survival_signals.py" -v`
- The slow headless runs: `MIMO_SLOW_TESTS=1 python3 -m unittest discover -s backend/tests -p "test_survival_sim.py"` (a few minutes), the same with `-p "test_survival_days.py"` and `-p "test_survival_expedition_run.py"`
- The worldgen fixture: `python3 -m backend.scripts.worldgen_fixture` (rewrites `shared/worldgen-fixture.json`; about 10 seconds)
- Frontend tests: `cd frontend && npm test` (321 pass at `254c452`; 335 after Task 13)
- One frontend test file: `cd frontend && npx vitest run src/survival/workshop.test.ts`
- Frontend build (type check + bundle): `cd frontend && npm run build`
- Lint touched files: `cd frontend && npx eslint src/survival src/engine` (prints nothing when clean)

Each task says how many tests it adds. If the starting totals differ (Mind's M1 and M2 land first), expect the same increases on top of them.

Transcription: every edit is a fenced block, either a whole new file ("Create `path`:") or a "replace: … with: …" pair in a named file ("In `path`, replace:", then "and replace:" for more pairs in the same file; code added at the end of a file is a pair on the file's last lines). Old texts are kept short and unique in their file, so each task still applies when a neighbouring line changes. The dry run applied each task with the controller's apply script (`.superpowers/sdd/2026-09-25-bond/apply_plan.py`) to a `git archive` copy of `254c452`, regenerated the fixture after Task 2, and ran the task's checks after each one; see resolution 1.

## Plan-level resolutions

The spec leaves the details to the plan. These are the details; every task follows them and the controller ledgers them.

1. **Base and dry run.** `254c452`: L4a, L4b and Bond B1 complete (Tasks 1–6, the checkpoint fix wave and its follow-up, with the new hooks in `talk.py`, `replies.py`, `events.py` and `owner_facts.py`). Bond B2 and B3 have not landed, and this plan uses none of their code. Mind (memory and teaching) is built before Making; this plan was written and dry-run without it, and resolution 15 lists every anchor Mind might also touch. The controller rebases those pairs once Mind lands and re-runs the dry run on the branch before executing. Dry run: each task applied in order to a `git archive` copy of `254c452` by the apply script, the fixture regenerated after Task 2, and after each task its focused tests, the whole backend suite and, for Tasks 1, 2, 6, 7, 11, 13 and 14, the frontend tests, build and lint; the slow headless runs after Tasks 5 and 14. Every pair applied. After every task its own tests and the whole backend suite passed (1,274 tests at the base, 1,357 after Task 14, 2 skipped throughout); the viewer's tests (321 at the base, 335 after Task 13), the build and the linter passed after every viewer task; the fixture regenerated to 39,652 cells; and the slow headless runs passed after Task 5 and again after Task 14 (`test_survival_sim.py` 7 tests, `test_survival_days.py` 3, `test_survival_expedition_run.py` 2), with no threshold changed. No wall-clock test failed on the way. The same tasks were first run on `c549b4b`; Bond's re-review at `254c452` added a test that every goal line is grammatical, which made every milestone of Making's goals a verb phrase and the first circuits' goal "First circuits". As Mind's first commits landed, all 14 tasks were also applied to `20e63a5` (Mind's memory stream and sleep, M1 in progress): every pair applied and the backend suite passed (1,382 tests).
2. **The new blocks and items.** T1 (Task 1), 18 blocks after L3's `creature_sprout`: `bookshelf`; `wool_orange`, `wool_pink`, `wool_yellow`; `rug_orange`, `rug_pink`, `rug_yellow` (flat); `kiln` (glows at its mouth, like a furnace); `stairs` (a see-through cube with a stepped side); `slab` (half high); `glass_pane`, `iron_bars` (see-through cubes, solid); `trapdoor` (flat); `flower_pot`, `sign`, `candle` (sprites, light 12 for the candle); `barrel`; `composter`. T2 (Task 7), 17 blocks after `candle`: `copper_wire` and `copper_wire_lit` (flat), `lever` and `lever_on`, `button` and `button_on` (sprites), `pressure_plate` (flat), `daylight_sensor` (a slab), `repeater`, `inverter`, `joiner` and their `_lit` blocks (flat, the dots on each tell them apart: two, one, three), `lamp` and `lamp_lit` (solid; light 15 when lit), `bell` (a sprite). A lit part is its own block that glows (`glow`), so the engine draws "on" by placing it, the viewer's block sync carries it, and both drop the plain item. Items: `paper`, `book`, the three dyes, `tallow`. Every tile is a recipe in `blocks.json` painted by `atlas.ts` in M3's soft-pixel style (20 new painters: books, rug, kiln, stair side, pane, trapdoor, bars, pot, sign, staves, slats, candle, wire, lever, button, plate, sensor, gate, lamp, bell). New shapes: `slab` (height 0.5) and `flat` (a sixteenth), drawn at their height by the mesher (their tops always drawn); the rest use L3's `cube` or the sprite layer. Wire, plates and rugs are not solid: Mimo walks over them.
3. **Clay and sand on the shores (the one worldgen change).** The spec keeps worldgen "untouched apart from the new ores", but it also asks for clay bricks and glass panes from sand, and no clay was generated anywhere. The smallest change that gives Mimo clay: where the generator lays gravel on a shore, `hash32(x, 0, z, seed, SHORE_LOOSE)` (channel 94) makes it clay 1 time in 5 and sand 1 time in 5. Heights never change; the legacy clearing keeps every block (231,562 surface cells within 192 blocks of the origin compared before and after: none differ); outside it, about 1 surface column in 1,300 changes from gravel to clay or sand, in new and existing worlds alike (never a cell Mimo changed). Both languages change together and the fixture samples shore clay and sand (the L3 plan's parity rule).
4. **Recipes.** Paper: 3 sugar cane make 3 (at a table). Book: 3 paper and a leather. Dyes: a flower makes 2. Coloured wool: wool and a dye. Rugs: 2 wool make 3. Bookshelf: 6 planks and a book. Kiln: 3 bricks and 5 cobblestone. Stairs: 6 planks make 4. Slabs: 3 planks make 6. Glass panes: 6 glass make 16. Trapdoors: 6 planks make 2. Iron bars: 6 ingots make 16. Flower pot: 3 bricks. Signs: 6 planks and a stick make 3. Barrel: 6 planks and 2 slabs. Composter: 7 slabs. Candle: tallow and string. A kiln fires clay into bricks and sand into glass and burns no fuel; a furnace still fires both with fuel. Sheep drop tallow half the time. Wiring (Task 9): an ingot makes 12 copper wire (at a table); lever: a stick and a cobblestone; button: a plank; pressure plate: 2 planks; daylight sensor: 3 glass, 3 slabs and a wire; repeater: 3 cobblestone, 2 torches and a wire; inverter: a torch and a wire; joiner: 3 cobblestone, a torch and 2 wires; lamp: an ingot and a torch; bell: 2 ingots and a stick. Copper is cheap on purpose: the computer (121 parts) takes 16 ingots.
5. **Making what a project needs** (`making.py`). A project registers in `making.NEEDS` a function of the Situation giving what it still wants made (`{"kiln": 1}`). `raw_needs` works back through the recipes to what Mimo lacks and gathers itself (sugar cane, flowers, clay, sand) or mines (copper ore, coal); the rest (leather, wool, tallow, string) waits for a hunt or the chest. `gather_materials` (45 + diligence / 10, minus late) digs or picks them from the nearest source in sight (never under water, never what Mimo built). `craft_plan` makes the items where Mimo stands: stations within reach are used, others are carried or made, put down beside it and taken back after (as `craft_tools` does); a crafting table first when a station must be made. The chest keeps off what a project needs (`storage.KEEPS_MORE`); mine_ore goes after copper (`work.MORE_ORES`); gather_stone digs a stack of cobblestone more at most (`work.MORE_STONE`, new); flowers a project wants are not junk.
6. **The workshop** (`workshop.py`). A first shelter's design (3×3 inside, a door, windows) on the nearest site within 16 blocks of home, fitted out: a slab roof with a trapdoor hatch, the crafting table and barrel in the bed's and chest's corners, the furnace and kiln in the front corners, a seat (a stair), iron bars in the windows, a sign by the door and torches at the corners. `build_workshop` (55 + creativity / 10) raises the walls and roof (making the roof's slabs first), then puts in the fixtures, four a batch; the walls standing make it done ("Pip built Pip's Workshop."). The goal "A workshop" follows a home and iron tools (the bars), rules score 40 + creativity / 10 + diligence / 20, with four milestones (bricks for the kiln, walls and roof, the four fixtures the spec names, the rest). Its walls are what gathering aims for while it is under way (`building.MORE_BLOCKS`, new).
7. **The cozy home** (`cozy.py`). The touches go on the home Mimo lives in, from its own design: a glass pane in each side window (taking a wall block out first when the home has none), a bookshelf in the back wall, a rug of its favourite colour on the passage, a flower pot and a candle inside the front corners, a sign by the door, a composter at the corner of the farm. `decorate_home` (50 + creativity / 10) makes up to four a batch outside and puts them in from inside. The favourite colour (orange, pink or yellow) is a trait fixed by the world seed and the name. Waking at dawn in a home with a bookshelf adds 5 mood ("content"). Crops within 4 blocks of a composter grow each stage a quarter quicker. The goal "A cozy home" follows a home, rules score 35 + creativity / 5, reward 20 mood; while it lacks leather, wool or tallow Mimo hunts though fed (`hunting.HUNT_FOR`).
8. **The signal engine** (`signals.py`). A circuit is a list of parts laid out from a machine's rows (`parse`). Each step (0.5 game seconds) is synchronous and deterministic: sources are read (a lever while thrown; a button for 2 steps; a pressure plate while Mimo stands on it or stood there in the last second; a daylight sensor by day, off in `NIGHT_PHASES`), then only the wire nets fed by something that changed are worked out again, breadth first from their feeders (power 15 at a feeder, one less a block, so 15 blocks), then the gates and outputs that read a change. A repeater passes its input on after 1 to 4 steps and gives full power; an inverter is NOT, one step later; a joiner ANDs or ORs its two sides, one step later; a gate reads only from behind (a joiner from its sides) and powers only what it faces. A lamp is lit while powered, a door open while powered (the viewer swings it), a bell rings once as it becomes powered (a routine "bell" event). The bound: before each step `step_bound` counts every cell the step could work out (each net fed by a change, every gate and output reading it or a change, the repeaters with a signal under way), and a machine takes the step only while that fits in what is left of MAX_CELLS = 256 this tick; a machine that runs out waits for the next tick (the machines take turns going first, `state["signal_turn"]`, so a busy clock never starves the computer), and one more than 240 steps behind skips ahead. A quiet machine (no source changed, nothing under way) costs nothing. The first run of a machine settles it (repeaters filled with what they read, an inverter set to start on held on, and the machine's own starting outputs held: `STARTS`) outside the budget, once. State is a `machine_signals` row a machine, written only when it changed; the lit blocks are placed through `Grid.put`, so the viewer's block sync draws them. The tick calls `run_signals` last in its transaction, after renewal and the creatures, while Mimo lives.
9. **Knowing how** (`tinker.py`). The four lessons are L4b journal lessons (`journal.LESSONS`, kind "making"): `copper_spark` ("Copper carries a spark"), `clock`, `latch` and `adder` (counting in twos), so the journal shows them, the viewer labels them "Making", and Mind's teaching makes them teachable without any code of Making's. Wiring waits for `copper_spark`; each thinking machine for its lesson. Mimo's own ways in: an old manual when it mines copper ore at y 0 or lower before it knows the spark, 1 time in 6 (a roll on the seed and the cell: "Pip found an old manual in the copper seam.", until L5's ruins carry them); and `tinker` (35 + curiosity / 4, by day, by home): with copper to hand it makes a lever, a wire and a lamp, lays them in a row, throws the lever and fiddles for 20 game seconds, and works it out with a chance of 0.25 + curiosity / 200 (the lamp and wire light up); each later lesson it works out by watching the machine before (the clock from the night-light, the latch from the clock, the adder from the memory cell). A session that finds nothing rests half a game day.
10. **Machines** (`machines.py`). A machine is a structure of kind "machine" whose design places its parts from a rule layout and carries its circuit (style "circuit"). Where it goes: a yard by the workshop (or home) within 12 blocks (24 for the counter and the computer, every other spot tried), by home's door for the night-light, or home's own door for the automatic door (plates in front and inside). A yard need not be flat (T3, Task 12): of the spots within reach whose footprint and the walkway round it are clear of water and of anything Mimo built, the one with the least digging and filling plus its distance is chosen; a column up to 2 blocks above the floor is dug down and one up to 2 below filled with dirt, and what grows there comes out (a tree's trunk whole, up to 5 above the floor, the leaves over it left to decay; a cactus, a pumpkin or a melon). The dry run's survey of 24 random worlds found a site for the computer within 24 blocks in 22 (on flat ground alone, 9); in the other two even the clock found none (open water). `build_machine` (55 + creativity / 10, by day, by home) works on the next machine of a making goal whose lesson Mimo knows: up to 12 parts a batch, made at the workshop's stations when it has one; the last part in makes it done ("Pip built a lamp on a lever."), and one more batch tries it out (the `flip` step throws its lever or presses its first button). The spec lists the seven machines as the milestones of one goal; the plan splits them in two so each goal's progress means something, and every milestone is a verb phrase (Bond B1's goal line reads the first one left: "I'm working toward first circuits: 40% done. Next: build a lamp on a lever."): **"First circuits"** (after the workshop: copper mined, the spark, a lamp on a lever, an automatic door, a night-light; rules score 40 + curiosity / 10 + creativity / 10) and **"A thinking machine"** (after the first circuits: the clock lesson, a clock, the latch, a memory cell, the adder, a counter, the computer; rules score 45 + curiosity / 10 + creativity / 10 + diligence / 20, reward 30). The first circuits' lamp gives light 15, so the night-light lights home's door.
11. **The thinking machine** (`computer.py`). Clock: an inverter and three repeaters of delay 2 in a loop, 7 steps on and 7 off (a lamp blinks every 7 game seconds). Memory cell: two inverters feeding each other, set and reset buttons and a lamp; Mimo presses set and the lamp stays lit. Counter: four stages, each a pair of repeaters that lock each other from the side (a repeater or joiner facing into a repeater's side locks it, as in Minecraft: the one engine rule T3 adds) and an inverter, so a stage flips once for each rise of its clock and its turned-over output clocks the next; a slower clock (repeaters of delay 4, a tick every 13 game seconds, longer than a count takes to ripple) drives it; four lamps count 0 to 15 and round. Mimo's computer: the same four stages driven by a daylight sensor (an inverter makes its turned-over clock), so each dawn counts one; a bell by the sensor rings at dawn; the lamps show through four joiners that AND each bit with the lever's line (lever off: dark). When it first runs, `STARTS["computer"]` sets it to the days Mimo has lived before today's dawn (by night, today's), so it shows the game day in binary, the highest bit west ("day 13 = 1101"); 4 bits count to 15 and round again. Throwing its lever the first time is a notable "computer" event and the thought "I built a machine that remembers how long I've been alive!". `READOUTS` give the viewer the count the counter and computer hold (`{value, bits, shown}`).
12. **Costs.** Parts and copper: a lamp on a lever 4 parts (1 ingot); clock 11 (2); memory cell 11 (2); counter 86 (11); computer 121 (16: 119 wire, 4 lamps, a bell). Engine: no step of any machine touches more than 64 cells (a quarter of the budget: tested for every machine over 600 steps with its sources toggled); the headless run of the computer over 17 game days, a tick every 60 game seconds, never takes 50 ms in a tick.
13. **The viewer.** Blocks: every new tile painted, the thin shapes drawn at their height, lit parts glowing (their own blocks). `/api/mimo` has `workshop`: the workshop (`name`, `status`), the machines oldest first (`id`, `name`, `machine`, `status`, `x`, `y`, `z`, `lamps` lit, and the counter's and computer's `readout`) and `doors_open` (the doors a machine holds open, which `SurvivalDoors` swings open). The HUD names the new purposes and the flip, shows the caption "Pip's computer: day 13 = 1101" under the home line while the computer's lamps are shown (after day 15, "0001, day 17 counted to 15 and round"), and a **Workshop** link beside the journal's once Mimo made something opens "What Pip has made": the workshop and each machine, working or being built, with its lamps lit or its count. The journal labels the making lessons "Making".
14. **Bond and Mind.** This plan calls no Bond or Mind code. What they gain without it: Bond B1's `replies.py` journal line reads `journal.LESSONS`, so Mimo can say what it learned about copper; B2's requests (when they land) map the owner's words to any registered goal in `goals.GOALS`, so "people build computers out of redstone" or "build a computer" maps to `thinking_machine` once the first circuits are done (the spec's owner-suggested goal); Mind makes every lesson in `journal.LESSONS` teachable by chat, so the four making lessons are teachable with no code of Making's. The "computer" event is notable (not in `ROUTINE_EVENTS`), so B3's `inbox.MIRRORS` can carry it (spec gaps).
15. **Registries and anchors.** New hooks are lists and dicts other modules append to: `making.NEEDS`, `work.MORE_ORES`, `work.MORE_STONE`, `building.MORE_BLOCKS`, `machines.MACHINES` and `MACHINE_GOALS`, `signals.STARTS` and `READOUTS`; existing ones used: `storage.KEEPS_MORE`, `hunting.HUNT_FOR`, `structures.STANDS_IN`, `crafting.RECIPES`, `renewal`'s growth, `light.BLOCK_LIGHT`, the purposes, steps and goals registries. Existing files change in a few short places: `crafting.py`, `worldgen.py` and `worldgen.ts`, `steps.py`, `storage.py`, `work.py`, `blueprints.py`, `building.py`, `structures.py`, `light.py`, `renewal.py`, `creatures/kinds.py`, `world.py`, `tick.py`, `snapshot.py`, `brain.py` (one import line a module), and in the viewer `blocks.ts`, `mesher.ts`, `atlas.ts`, `hud.ts`, `doors.ts`, `journal.ts`, `animation.ts`, `types.ts`, `SurvivalDoors.tsx`, `WorldCanvas.tsx`, `SurvivalHud.tsx` and `SurvivalWorld.tsx`.
   **Anchors Mind might also touch** (the controller rebases these after Mind lands; each is one short line, and every other anchor is in a file Mind has no reason to change):
   - `backend/survival/journal.py` (its `LESSONS` block): not touched. `tinker.py` registers the four lessons with `journal.teach(Lesson(thing, kind, words, fact, unlocks, lines), …)`, L4b's six fields in order, and calls `learn_lesson`, `taught` and `journal_ready` by name. If Mind changes `Lesson`'s fields or `teach`, only that one `teach(...)` call in Task 10 changes.
   - `backend/survival/goals.py`: not touched. The four goals are `register_goal(Goal(key, title, why, milestones, score=…, thought=…, after=…, reward=…))` with `Milestone(text, share, purposes, recipes)`, as L4a's are.
   - Brain wiring, `backend/survival/brain.py`: Task 3's pair adds Making's import line before `from backend.survival.curiosity import note_discoveries, tend_curiosity`, and Tasks 4, 5, 9, 10 and 12 grow that line and the ones after it; Task 5 calls `tend_comfort` before `    asleep = (state.get("action") or {}).get("kind") == "sleep"` in `notice_step`; Tasks 10 and 12 call `observe_tinker` and `observe_computer` before `    observe_expedition(state, step, context, at)` in `observe_step`.
   - `backend/survival/world.py`: the signal table's import before `from backend.survival.steps import WORKSTATIONS`, `create_signal_table(db)` after `    create_bond_tables(db)` (where Mind's `mind_memories` table is likely created too), and `"bell"` on `ROUTINE_EVENTS`'s last line (Task 8).
   - `backend/survival/snapshot.py`: the `workshop_view` import after `from backend.survival.journal import journal_view`, `workshop = workshop_view(db)` after `        journal = journal_view(db, state.get("brain"))`, and the `workshop` field after `        "journal": journal,` (Task 11). Mind's memory fields are likely to land beside these.
   - `backend/survival/tick.py`: the docstring's last paragraph, the import after `from backend.survival.script import rest_plan`, and `run_signals` in an `else:` after `if caught(state): record_death(…)` (Task 8).
   - The viewer: `journal.ts`'s `KIND_LABELS` line and `types.ts`'s `JournalEntry.kind` line (Task 11: the kind "making"), `types.ts`'s `expedition?: Expedition | null` (the `workshop` field after it), `SurvivalHud.tsx` and `SurvivalWorld.tsx` (Tasks 11 and 13: the doors, the caption, the Workshop link and panel), and the README's `## Current world rules` heading (the Making section goes before it).
16. **Out of scope** (spec gaps): two-storey homes and stairs to a loft; barrels as storage (a barrel is a fixture); signs that show a name (B2's naming); the "improve the machine" goal (8 bits, a calculator); drawing a gate's facing in the viewer; sounds.

| Path | Status | Responsibility |
|------|--------|----------------|
| `shared/blocks.json` | Modify | 38 tiles and 35 blocks at the end of their lists |
| `frontend/src/engine/atlas.ts`, `blocks.ts`, `mesher.ts` | Modify | 20 tile painters; the `slab` and `flat` heights; drawing thin blocks at their height |
| `backend/services/worldgen.py`, `frontend/src/engine/worldgen.ts`, `backend/scripts/worldgen_fixture.py`, `shared/worldgen-fixture.json` | Modify | Clay and sand among the shore gravel; sampled; regenerated |
| `backend/services/crafting.py` | Modify | The T1 and T2 recipes; the kiln fires clay and sand without fuel |
| `backend/survival/making.py` | Create | What projects need, the raw materials, `craft_plan`, `place_steps`, `gather_materials`, the favourite colour |
| `backend/survival/workshop.py` | Create | The workshop: design, `build_workshop`, the goal |
| `backend/survival/cozy.py` | Create | The cozy touches, `decorate_home`, the bookshelf's content morning, the goal |
| `backend/survival/signals.py` | Create | The signal engine: layouts, circuits, steps, the bound, the `machine_signals` table, `run_signals` |
| `backend/survival/machines.py` | Create | Machines: designs, `build_machine`, the `flip` step, the first circuits goal, `workshop_view` |
| `backend/survival/tinker.py` | Create | The four making lessons, the old manual, `tinker` |
| `backend/survival/computer.py` | Create | The clock, memory cell, counter and computer; its start and readout; the thinking machine goal |
| `backend/survival/steps.py`, `storage.py`, `work.py`, `blueprints.py`, `building.py`, `structures.py`, `light.py`, `renewal.py`, `creatures/kinds.py`, `world.py`, `tick.py`, `snapshot.py`, `brain.py` | Modify | The kiln as a station; hooks; slabs and fixtures in building; light 12 and 15; the composter; tallow; the table; the tick; the `workshop` field; imports |
| `backend/tests/test_blocks_{making,wiring}.py`, `test_survival_{making,workshop,cozy,signals,machines,tinker,workshop_view,computer}.py` | Create | One file per part |
| `frontend/src/survival/workshop.ts` (+ test), `WorkshopPanel.tsx` | Create | The caption and the panel's rows; the panel |
| `frontend/src/survival/types.ts`, `hud.ts`, `doors.ts`, `journal.ts`, `animation.ts`, `SurvivalDoors.tsx`, `WorldCanvas.tsx`, `SurvivalHud.tsx`, `SurvivalWorld.tsx` | Modify | The `workshop` field; words; doors held open; the "Making" label; the flip; the caption and the Workshop link |
| `README.md` | Modify | "Making" |

## Tasks

**T1: New blocks and crafts**
1. New blocks and their soft-pixel textures
2. Clay and sand on the shores
3. Recipes, the kiln and gathering materials
4. The workshop
5. A cozy home
6. Viewer: the words for making

**T2: Wiring**

8. The signal engine
9. Machines and the first circuits
10. Knowing how: the lessons, the old manual and tinkering
11. The workshop view, and the viewer's doors, words and journal

**T3: The thinking machine**
12. The clock, the memory cell, the counter and Mimo's computer
13. Viewer: the computer's caption and the Workshop panel
14. Manual check on the demo and the README

Tasks 1–5, 7–10 and 12 are the backend (1 and 7 draw their blocks too); 6, 11 and 13 the viewer. Each milestone works on its own: T1 without T2 gives the blocks, the workshop and the cozy home; T2 without T3 gives the engine and the first circuits.

# T1: New blocks and crafts

### Task 1: New blocks and their soft-pixel textures

T1's 18 blocks go at the end of the registry, after L3's `creature_sprout`, so no id changes, each with tiles painted in M3's soft-pixel style by 12 new painters (resolution 2). Two new shapes draw thin blocks at their height: a slab half high, rugs and trapdoors a sixteenth, their tops drawn even under a block. Nothing makes them yet: Task 3 adds the recipes.

**Files:**
- Modify: `frontend/src/engine/atlas.ts`, `frontend/src/engine/blocks.ts`, `frontend/src/engine/mesher.ts`, `shared/blocks.json`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_blocks_bigger_world.py`, `backend/tests/test_blocks_making.py`, `frontend/src/engine/atlas.test.ts`, `frontend/src/engine/blocks.test.ts`, `frontend/src/engine/mesher.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `blocks.ts`: `SLAB_HEIGHT`, `FLAT_HEIGHT`, `HEIGHT_BY_ID`

Create `backend/tests/test_blocks_making.py`:

```python
import unittest

from backend.services.blocks import BLOCK_IDS, BLOCK_LIST, TILES, hardness, is_replaceable, is_solid, mining_tool
from backend.services.crafting import BLOCKS
from backend.tests.test_blocks_bigger_world import BIGGER_WORLD

# Making's T1 blocks, in registry order right after L3's.
MAKING = ("bookshelf", "wool_orange", "wool_pink", "wool_yellow", "rug_orange", "rug_pink", "rug_yellow", "kiln",
          "stairs", "slab", "glass_pane", "trapdoor", "iron_bars", "flower_pot", "sign", "barrel", "composter",
          "candle")
AXE = ("bookshelf", "stairs", "slab", "trapdoor", "sign", "barrel", "composter")
THIN = ("rug_orange", "rug_pink", "rug_yellow", "trapdoor", "flower_pot", "sign", "candle")


def shape(name: str) -> str | None:
    return BLOCK_LIST[BLOCK_IDS[name]].get("shape")


class MakingBlockTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_the_new_blocks_follow_the_bigger_worlds_so_older_ids_never_change(self):
        names = [block["name"] for block in BLOCK_LIST]
        start = BLOCK_IDS["bookshelf"]
        self.assertEqual(start, BLOCK_IDS[BIGGER_WORLD[-1]] + 1)
        self.assertEqual(names[start:start + len(MAKING)], list(MAKING))
        self.assertLess(len(BLOCK_LIST), 255)

    def test_every_new_face_has_a_tile(self):
        for name in MAKING:
            textures = BLOCK_LIST[BLOCK_IDS[name]]["textures"]
            for tile in [textures] if isinstance(textures, str) else textures.values():
                self.assertIn(tile, TILES, name)

    def test_each_drops_itself_and_breaks_fastest_with_its_tool(self):
        for name in MAKING:
            self.assertEqual(BLOCKS[name]["drop"], name)
            self.assertIsNotNone(hardness(name), name)
            self.assertFalse(is_replaceable(name), name)
        for name in AXE:
            self.assertEqual(mining_tool(name), "axe", name)
        self.assertEqual((mining_tool("kiln"), mining_tool("iron_bars")), ("pickaxe", "pickaxe"))

    def test_rugs_trapdoors_pots_signs_and_candles_let_mimo_through(self):
        for name in THIN:
            self.assertFalse(is_solid(name), name)
        for name in ("bookshelf", "wool_pink", "kiln", "stairs", "slab", "glass_pane", "iron_bars", "barrel",
                     "composter"):
            self.assertTrue(is_solid(name), name)

    def test_the_viewer_draws_the_thin_ones_by_shape(self):
        for name in ("rug_orange", "rug_pink", "rug_yellow", "trapdoor"):
            self.assertEqual(shape(name), "flat", name)
        self.assertEqual(shape("slab"), "slab")
        for name in ("stairs", "glass_pane", "iron_bars", "composter"):
            self.assertEqual(shape(name), "cube", name)
        for name in MAKING:
            self.assertNotIn("shape", BLOCKS[name])  # how a block is drawn is not gameplay

    def test_the_kiln_and_the_candle_glow(self):
        self.assertTrue(BLOCKS["kiln"]["glow"] and BLOCKS["candle"]["glow"])
        self.assertNotIn("glow", BLOCKS["bookshelf"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```


with:

```ts
  // Making (T1): books on shelves, rugs, a kiln's glowing mouth, stairs, windows, a trapdoor, iron bars,
  // a flower pot, a sign, a barrel, a composter and a candle.
  books: ({ color, accent }, random) => {
    const spines: Rgb[] = [accent ?? color, [92, 116, 150], [110, 146, 96], [206, 172, 92]]
    const book = Array.from({ length: N }, () => spines[Math.floor(random() * spines.length)])
    return grid((i, j) => {
      if (j === 0 || j === N - 1 || j === 4 || i === 0 || i === N - 1) return tone(color, 0.82 * jitter(random))
      return tone(book[(i + (j > 4 ? 3 : 0)) % N], (j === 1 || j === 5 ? 1.08 : 1) * jitter(random, 0.06))
    })
  },
  rug: ({ color, accent }, random) => grid((i, j) =>
    edge(i, j) ? tone(accent ?? color, jitter(random)) : tone(color, ((i + j) % 4 === 0 ? 0.9 : 1) * jitter(random))),
  kiln: ({ color, accent }, random) => {
    const bricks = PATTERNS.bricks({ pattern: 'bricks', color, accent: [214, 200, 184] }, random)
    return bricks.map((pixel, index) => {
      const i = index % N, j = Math.floor(index / N)
      return i >= 2 && i <= 5 && j >= 5 && j <= 6 ? tone(accent ?? color, jitter(random, 0.1)) : pixel
    })
  },
  stair_side: (recipe, random) => PATTERNS.planks(recipe, random).map((pixel, index) =>
    index % N < N / 2 && Math.floor(index / N) < N / 2 ? CLEAR : pixel),
  pane: ({ color }, random) => grid((i, j) => {
    if (edge(i, j)) return tone(color, 0.78 * jitter(random, 0.05))
    if (i === 3 || j === 3) return tone(color, 0.9 * jitter(random, 0.05))
    return i - j === 1 && i < 3 ? tone(color, 1.2, 220) : CLEAR
  }),
  trapdoor: ({ color }, random) => grid((i, j) => {
    if ((i === 2 || i === 5) && (j === 2 || j === 5)) return CLEAR
    return tone(color, (edge(i, j) ? 0.8 : j === 3 || j === 4 ? 0.92 : 1) * jitter(random))
  }),
  bars: ({ color }, random) => grid((i, j) => {
    const rail = j === 0 || j === N - 1
    return i % 3 === 1 || rail ? tone(color, (rail ? 0.85 : 1) * jitter(random, 0.05)) : CLEAR
  }),
  sprite_pot: ({ color, accent }, random) => grid((i, j) => {
    if (j === 5) return i >= 1 && i <= 6 ? tone(accent ?? color, 1.1 * jitter(random)) : CLEAR
    if (j > 5) return i >= 2 && i <= 5 ? tone(accent ?? color, (i === 5 ? 0.86 : 1) * jitter(random)) : CLEAR
    const stem = j >= 3 && (i === 3 || i === 4)
    const leaf = (j === 2 && i >= 2 && i <= 5) || (j === 1 && (i === 2 || i === 5))
    return stem || leaf ? tone(color, jitter(random, 0.12)) : CLEAR
  }),
  sprite_sign: ({ color, accent }, random) => grid((i, j) => {
    if (j >= 1 && j <= 4) {
      const words = (j === 2 || j === 3) && i >= 1 && i <= 6 && (i + j) % 3 !== 0
      return tone(words ? accent ?? color : color, (edge(i, j) ? 0.88 : 1) * jitter(random))
    }
    return j >= 5 && (i === 3 || i === 4) ? tone(accent ?? color, jitter(random)) : CLEAR
  }),
  staves: ({ color, accent }, random) => grid((i, j) =>
    j === 1 || j === N - 2 ? tone(accent ?? color, jitter(random, 0.05)) : tone(color, (i % 2 ? 0.88 : 1) * jitter(random))),
  slats: ({ color }, random) => grid((i, j) => {
    const post = i === 0 || i === N - 1
    return post || j % 3 !== 2 ? tone(color, (post ? 0.84 : 1) * jitter(random)) : CLEAR
  }),
  sprite_candle: ({ color, accent }, random) => grid((i, j) => {
    if (j >= 3) return i >= 3 && i <= 4 ? tone(color, (i === 4 ? 0.88 : 1) * jitter(random, 0.05)) : CLEAR
    if (j === 2) return i === 3 ? tone([60, 52, 44], 1) : CLEAR
    return (i === 3 || (j === 1 && i === 4)) ? tone(accent ?? color, 1.1 * jitter(random, 0.05)) : CLEAR
  }),
  glass: ({ color }, random) => grid((i, j) => {
```

```ts
          FACES.forEach((face, faceIndex) => {
            const neighbor = idAt(px + face.dir[0], layer + face.dir[1], pz + face.dir[2])
            if (neighbor === -1 || neighbor === id || LAYER_BY_ID[neighbor] === LAYER_OPAQUE) return
            const light = face.shade * blockTint
            cutout.quad(face.corners.map(([x, y, z]) => [wx + x, wy + y, wz + z] as Vec3),
              tileUv(faceTiles[id * 6 + faceIndex]), [light, light, light, light], false, glow ? 1 : 0)
```

with:

```ts
          // opaque block or one of their own, face-shaded, with no corner shadows. Making: a slab or a
          // flat plate is the same cube cut down to its height; its top always shows, and its sides
          // show the bottom of their tile.
          const height = HEIGHT_BY_ID[id]
          FACES.forEach((face, faceIndex) => {
            const neighbor = idAt(px + face.dir[0], layer + face.dir[1], pz + face.dir[2])
            const covered = neighbor === -1 || neighbor === id || LAYER_BY_ID[neighbor] === LAYER_OPAQUE
            if (covered && !(face.dir[1] === 1 && height < 1)) return
            const light = face.shade * blockTint
            const uv = tileUv(faceTiles[id * 6 + faceIndex])
            if (face.dir[1] === 0) uv[3] = uv[1] + (uv[3] - uv[1]) * height
            cutout.quad(face.corners.map(([x, y, z]) => [wx + x, wy + y * height, wz + z] as Vec3),
              uv, [light, light, light, light], false, glow ? 1 : 0)
```

with:

```python
def _shore_loose(seed: str, count: int, xs: range = FEATURE_XS) -> list[tuple[int, int]]:
    """Making: shore columns of clay and of sand (not a desert's), `count` of each."""
    seen: dict[str, int] = {}
    found = []
    for x in xs:
        for z in range(-60, 60, 2):
            if not shore(x, z, seed) or biome_at(x, z, seed) == "desert":
                continue
            kind = surface_material(x, z, seed)
            if kind in ("clay", "sand") and seen.get(kind, 0) < count:
                seen[kind] = seen.get(kind, 0) + 1
                found.append((x, z))
                if len(seen) == 2 and all(value == count for value in seen.values()):
                    return found
    return found


def _entrances(seed: str, count: int, rxs: range = range(4, 40)) -> list[dict]:
```

### Task 3: Recipes, the kiln and gathering materials

The T1 recipes (resolution 4), a kiln that fires clay and sand without fuel, tallow from sheep, and `making.py`: projects say what they want made (`NEEDS`), `raw_needs` works out the raw materials, `craft_plan` makes things where Mimo stands, and `gather_materials` digs clay and sand and picks sugar cane and flowers (resolution 5). mine_ore goes after copper and gather_stone digs more cobblestone while a project wants them, and the chest keeps what a project needs. No project registers yet: Tasks 4, 5 and 9 do.

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/survival/making.py`
- Modify: `backend/services/crafting.py`, `backend/survival/brain.py`, `backend/survival/creatures/kinds.py`, `backend/survival/steps.py`, `backend/survival/storage.py`, `backend/survival/work.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_survival_creatures.py`, `backend/tests/test_survival_making.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `services.crafting`: `COOKING`, `KILN_FIRED`, `RECIPES`; `services.worldgen`: `hash32`, `surface_material`, `terrain_height`; `backend.survival`: `storage`, `work`; `carrying`: `STACK`, `crafts_fit`; `foraging`: `reach_steps`, `whole_walk`; `grid`: `Cell`; `once`: `log_once`; `purposes`: `Purpose`, `late_penalty`, `register`; `senses`: `natural_plants`, `near_failure`; `situation`: `Situation`; `steps`: `REACH`, `STATION_REACH`, `WORKSTATIONS`; `structures`: `reserved`; `toolmaking`: `SMELTED`, `Short`, `make`, `place_station`, `station_spots`.
- Produces:
  - `services.crafting`: `KILN_FIRED`
  - `making`: `PLANTS`, `GROUND`, `GATHERED`, `MINED`, `SOURCE_SIGHT`, `PER_BATCH`, `GATHER_BATCHES`, `RAW_TRIES`, `COLOURS`, `COLOUR_CHANNEL`, `QUARRIED`, `NEEDS`, `favourite_colour(state) -> str`, `needs(s) -> dict[str, int]`, `raw_needs(s) -> dict[str, int]`, `ingredients(items) -> set[str]`, `kept_for_making(s, item) -> float`, `ores_for_making(s) -> list[str]`, `stations_for(steps, near, inventory) -> list[str]`, `craft_plan(s, items) -> list[dict] | None`, `place_steps(s, stands, jobs, at) -> list[dict]`, `gathered_wanted(s) -> dict[str, int]`, `diggable(s, cell, kind) -> bool`, `sources(s) -> list[tuple[Cell, str]]`, `gather_valid(s) -> bool`, `gather_facts(s) -> str`, `plan_gather(s, context) -> list[dict]`, `stone_for_making(s) -> float`
  - `work`: `MORE_STONE`, `MORE_ORES`, `more_ores(s) -> list[str]`

```python
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from backend.services.crafting import craft, smelt
from backend.survival import brain  # noqa: F401  (registers every purpose)
from backend.survival.making import (
    COLOURS, NEEDS, craft_plan, favourite_colour, needs, place_steps, raw_needs, sources,
)
from backend.survival.purposes import PURPOSES
from backend.survival.storage import junk, kept
from backend.survival.work import stone_goal, wanted_ores
from backend.tests.test_survival_building import NIGHT, World

FLAT = lambda x, z, seed: 0  # noqa: E731
CLAY = {(4, 0), (5, 0), (9, 3)}


def shore(x, z, seed):
    return "clay" if (x, z) in CLAY else "grass"


def want(test, items):
    """A project that wants `items` made and put in place while the test runs (making.NEEDS)."""
    wants = lambda s: dict(items)  # noqa: E731
    NEEDS.append(wants)
    test.addCleanup(NEEDS.remove, wants)


class RecipeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    CASES = {
        "paper": ({"sugar_cane": 3}, {"paper": 3}),
        "book": ({"paper": 3, "leather": 1}, {"book": 1}),
        "dye_pink": ({"flower_pink": 1}, {"dye_pink": 2}),
        "wool_pink": ({"wool": 1, "dye_pink": 1}, {"wool_pink": 1}),
        "rug_pink": ({"wool_pink": 2}, {"rug_pink": 3}),
        "bookshelf": ({"birch_planks": 6, "book": 1}, {"bookshelf": 1}),  # any wood's planks
        "kiln": ({"brick": 3, "cobblestone": 5}, {"kiln": 1}),
        "stairs": ({"planks": 6}, {"stairs": 4}),
        "slab": ({"planks": 3}, {"slab": 6}),
        "glass_pane": ({"glass": 6}, {"glass_pane": 16}),
        "trapdoor": ({"planks": 6}, {"trapdoor": 2}),
        "iron_bars": ({"iron_ingot": 6}, {"iron_bars": 16}),
        "flower_pot": ({"brick": 3}, {"flower_pot": 1}),
        "sign": ({"planks": 6, "sticks": 1}, {"sign": 3}),
        "barrel": ({"planks": 6, "slab": 2}, {"barrel": 1}),
        "composter": ({"slab": 7}, {"composter": 1}),
        "candle": ({"tallow": 1, "string": 1}, {"candle": 1}),
    }

    def test_the_new_recipes_make_what_they_say(self):
        for recipe, (inventory, made) in self.CASES.items():
            self.assertEqual(craft(inventory, recipe, {"crafting_table"}), made, recipe)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            craft({"sugar_cane": 3}, "paper", set())  # paper is made at a crafting table

    def test_a_kiln_fires_clay_and_sand_without_fuel_and_a_furnace_still_burns_some(self):
        self.assertEqual(smelt({"clay": 2}, "clay", {"kiln"}), {"clay": 1, "brick": 1})
        self.assertEqual(smelt({"sand": 1}, "sand", {"kiln"}), {"glass": 1})
        self.assertEqual(smelt({"clay": 1, "coal": 1}, "clay", {"furnace"}), {"brick": 1})
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            smelt({"clay": 1}, "clay", {"furnace"})  # no fuel
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            smelt({"iron_ore": 1, "coal": 1}, "iron_ore", {"kiln"})  # a kiln fires clay and sand only


class NeedsTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_raw_needs_work_out_clay_for_a_kiln_and_sand_for_windows(self):
        world = World({"cobblestone": 5, "oak_log": 4})
        self.assertEqual((needs(world.situation()), raw_needs(world.situation())), ({}, {}))
        want(self, {"kiln": 1})
        self.assertEqual(raw_needs(world.situation()), {"clay": 3})
        world.state["inventory"]["brick"] = 2
        self.assertEqual(raw_needs(world.situation()), {"clay": 1})
        want(self, {"glass_pane": 2})
        self.assertEqual(raw_needs(world.situation()), {"clay": 1, "sand": 6})
        self.assertEqual(needs(world.situation()), {"kiln": 1, "glass_pane": 2})

    def test_what_no_gathering_brings_leaves_a_thing_waiting(self):
        world = World({"planks": 6})
        want(self, {"bookshelf": 1})
        self.assertEqual(raw_needs(world.situation()), {"sugar_cane": 3})  # the leather waits for a hunt

    def test_the_chest_keeps_off_what_a_project_needs_and_its_flowers_are_not_junk(self):
        world = World({"clay": 3, "flower_pink": 2, **{f"item_{n}": 1 for n in range(9)}})
        s = world.situation()
        self.assertEqual(kept(s, "clay"), 0)
        self.assertIn(("flower_pink", 2), junk(s))
        want(self, {"kiln": 1, "rug_pink": 1})
        s = world.situation()
        self.assertEqual(kept(s, "clay"), 32)
        self.assertNotIn("flower_pink", [item for item, _ in junk(s)])

    def test_gather_stone_digs_for_the_cobblestone_making_wants_a_stack_at_most(self):
        world = World({"wooden_pickaxe": 1, "clay": 3, "oak_log": 2})
        self.assertEqual(stone_goal(world.situation()), 12)
        want(self, {"kiln": 1})
        self.assertEqual(raw_needs(world.situation())["cobblestone"], 5)
        self.assertEqual(stone_goal(world.situation()), 12 + 5)
        want(self, {"furnace": 6})
        self.assertEqual(stone_goal(world.situation()), 12 + 32)

    def test_mine_ore_goes_after_copper_while_making_wants_it(self):
        world = World({"stone_pickaxe": 1, "coal": 8, "iron_ore": 3})
        self.assertNotIn("copper_ore", wanted_ores(world.situation()))
        want(self, {"copper_ingot": 2})
        self.assertIn("copper_ore", wanted_ores(world.situation()))

    def test_the_favourite_colour_is_a_trait_fixed_by_the_seed_and_the_name(self):
        colour = favourite_colour({"name": "Pip", "world_seed": "1"})
        self.assertIn(colour, COLOURS)
        self.assertEqual(favourite_colour({"name": "Pip", "world_seed": "1"}), colour)
        self.assertGreater(len({favourite_colour({"name": name, "world_seed": "1"})
                                for name in ("Pip", "Pebble", "Moss", "Juniper", "Sol", "Wren")}), 1)


class MakingThingsTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_a_table_and_a_furnace_go_down_beside_mimo_and_come_back_after(self):
        world = World({"clay": 3, "cobblestone": 13, "oak_log": 4})
        steps = craft_plan(world.situation(), {"kiln": 1})
        places = [step for step in steps if step["kind"] == "place"]
        self.assertEqual([step["block"] for step in places], ["crafting_table", "furnace"])
        self.assertEqual([step["item"] for step in steps if step["kind"] == "smelt"], ["clay"] * 3)
        self.assertEqual([step["recipe"] for step in steps if step["kind"] == "craft"][-1], "kiln")
        self.assertEqual(steps[-2:], [{"kind": "mine", "target": places[1]["target"], "keep": True},
                                      {"kind": "mine", "target": places[0]["target"], "keep": True}])
        self.assertIsNone(craft_plan(world.situation(), {"kiln": 2}))  # not enough clay for two
        self.assertEqual(craft_plan(World({"kiln": 1}).situation(), {"kiln": 1}), [])  # carried already

    def test_stations_placed_within_reach_are_used_where_they_stand(self):
        world = World({"clay": 3, "cobblestone": 5, "planks": 3})
        world.grid.put(3, 1, 1, "crafting_table")
        world.grid.put(1, 1, 3, "kiln")
        steps = craft_plan(world.situation(), {"kiln": 1})
        self.assertEqual([step["kind"] for step in steps], ["smelt", "smelt", "smelt", "craft"])

    def test_place_steps_walk_to_the_nearest_stand_that_reaches(self):
        world = World(position=(1, 1, 1))
        place = {"kind": "place", "target": [8, 1, 0], "block": "lamp"}
        steps = place_steps(world.situation(), [(0, 1, 0), (6, 1, 0), (7, 1, 3)], [((8, 1, 0), [place])])
        self.assertEqual(steps, [{"kind": "walk", "target": [6, 1, 0], "reach": 0.0, "whole": True}, place])
        here = {"kind": "place", "target": [1, 1, 1], "block": "rug_pink"}
        self.assertEqual(place_steps(world.situation(), [(1, 1, 2)], [((1, 1, 1), [here])])[0]["target"], [1, 1, 2])


@patch("backend.survival.making.terrain_height", FLAT)
@patch("backend.survival.making.surface_material", shore)
class GatherTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.world = World({"cobblestone": 5, "oak_log": 4})
        for x, z in CLAY:
            self.world.grid.put(x, 0, z, "clay")
        self.world.grid.put(5, 1, 0, "water")  # this clay lies under water

    def test_it_digs_the_nearest_clay_a_project_wants_and_never_under_water(self):
        gather = PURPOSES["gather_materials"]
        self.assertFalse(gather.valid(self.world.situation()))  # nothing wanted
        want(self, {"kiln": 1})

        self.assertIn("wants 3 clay", gather.facts(s))
        self.assertEqual(gather.plan(s, self.world.context()), [
            {"kind": "mine", "target": [4, 0, 0]},
            {"kind": "walk", "target": [9, 0, 3], "reach": 2.0, "whole": True},
            {"kind": "mine", "target": [9, 0, 3]}])
        self.assertFalse(gather.valid(self.world.situation(NIGHT)))

    def test_sugar_cane_comes_down_from_the_top_of_its_stalk(self):
        for y in (1, 2, 3):
            self.world.grid.put(3, y, 0, "sugar_cane")
        want(self, {"bookshelf": 1})
        with patch("backend.survival.making.natural_plants", lambda seed, x, z, radius, kinds: [(3, 1, 0)]):
            s = self.world.situation()
            self.assertEqual([cell for cell, _ in sources(s)], [(3, 3, 0), (3, 2, 0), (3, 1, 0)])
            self.assertEqual([step["target"] for step in PURPOSES["gather_materials"].plan(s, self.world.context())],
                             [[3, 3, 0], [3, 2, 0], [3, 1, 0]])


if __name__ == "__main__":
```

Create `backend/survival/making.py`:

```python
"""Making (T1): the raw materials Mimo gathers for what it makes, and how it makes it.

The owner, 2026-09-25: "continue making the world more complex through more block types or objects to
create". Several projects want things made and put in place: the workshop (backend.survival.workshop),
the touches that make home cozy (backend.survival.cozy) and, from T2, the machines
(backend.survival.machines). Each registers in NEEDS a function of the Situation giving the blocks
and items its next steps still want ({"kiln": 1, "glass_pane": 2}); `needs` adds them up once per
Situation.

`raw_needs` works out through the recipes (toolmaking.make) the raw materials those take that Mimo
does not carry: the ones it gathers itself (GATHERED: sugar cane and flowers, clay and sand) and the
ores mine_ore brings (MINED: copper ore). What else a project wants (leather for a book, wool, tallow,
string) waits for a hunt or the chest; a thing it cannot make yet is left for later.

- gather_materials, "gather materials": picks or digs what raw_needs asks for from sources within
  SOURCE_SIGHT blocks, nearest first: plants worldgen grew that still stand (sugar cane from the top
  of its stalk down) and clay or sand on the ground with open air over it, never under water, never
  what Mimo built or tends, never near a failed step. Up to PER_BATCH a batch and GATHER_BATCHES
  batches a choice. Day work in the work band: 45 plus a tenth of diligence, minus late.
- mine_ore goes after copper ore while raw_needs asks for it (work.MORE_ORES), and gather_stone digs for
  the cobblestone it asks for (QUARRIED), a stack at most beyond its own goal (work.MORE_STONE: more
  would only go in the chest, where making cannot use it).
- The chest does not take what a project needs (storage.KEEPS_MORE): the items NEEDS names and every
  ingredient of their recipes stay on Mimo, so the clay it dug is still there when the kiln is made.

`craft_plan(s, items)` is the craft and smelt steps that make `items` from what Mimo carries, with
the stations the recipes need (a crafting table; a furnace, or a kiln for clay and sand): used as
they stand when one is placed within STATION_REACH, else carried or made, put down beside Mimo and
mined back after (`keep`), as craft_tools does. None when something is missing or would not fit.
`place_steps(s, stands, jobs)` walks to the stand nearest where Mimo will be that reaches each job's
cell, when it is out of reach, and does the job.

Mimo's favourite colour (orange, pink or yellow) is a trait of the life, fixed by its world seed and
its name (`favourite_colour`): the rug it makes for home is of that colour (backend.survival.cozy).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import math
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from backend.services.crafting import COOKING, KILN_FIRED, RECIPES
from backend.services.worldgen import hash32, surface_material, terrain_height
from backend.survival import storage, work
from backend.survival.carrying import STACK, crafts_fit
from backend.survival.foraging import reach_steps, whole_walk
from backend.survival.grid import Cell
from backend.survival.once import log_once
from backend.survival.purposes import Purpose, late_penalty, register
from backend.survival.senses import natural_plants, near_failure
from backend.survival.situation import Situation
from backend.survival.steps import REACH, STATION_REACH, WORKSTATIONS
from backend.survival.structures import reserved
from backend.survival.toolmaking import SMELTED, Short, make, place_station, station_spots

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from backend.survival.actions import ActionContext

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

PLANTS = ("sugar_cane", "flower_orange", "flower_pink", "flower_yellow")
GROUND = ("clay", "sand")
GATHERED = PLANTS + GROUND
MINED = ("copper_ore",)
SOURCE_SIGHT = 24
PER_BATCH = 8
GATHER_BATCHES = 3
RAW_TRIES = 64  # units of raw material one wanted item may add before raw_needs stops counting
COLOURS = ("orange", "pink", "yellow")
COLOUR_CHANNEL = 95
QUARRIED = ("cobblestone",)  # what gather_stone digs

# Functions of the Situation giving {item: count}: blocks and items a project still wants made and put
# in place (the workshop's, the cozy home's, the machines').
NEEDS: list = []


def favourite_colour(state: dict) -> str:
    """Orange, pink or yellow: a trait of the life, fixed by its world seed and its name."""
    salt = sum(map(ord, state.get("name", "")))
    return COLOURS[hash32(salt, 0, 0, state.get("world_seed", "0"), COLOUR_CHANNEL) % len(COLOURS)]


def needs(s: Situation) -> dict[str, int]:
    """What every project still wants made and put in place, added up (one that crashes wants nothing,
    logged once), read once per Situation."""
    def look() -> dict[str, int]:
        total: dict[str, int] = {}
        for wants in NEEDS:
            try:
                for item, count in wants(s).items():
                    if count > 0:
                        total[item] = total.get(item, 0) + int(count)
            except Exception as error:
                log_once(logger, "making needs", error)
        return total
    return s.sensed("making needs", look)


def raw_needs(s: Situation) -> dict[str, int]:
    """The raw materials (GATHERED and MINED) the recipes for `needs` take that Mimo does not carry,
    each wanted item worked out in turn on what the ones before it left (read once per Situation)."""
    def look() -> dict[str, int]:
        trial: dict[str, int] = dict(s.inventory)
        raw: dict[str, int] = {}
        for item, count in sorted(needs(s).items()):
            for _ in range(RAW_TRIES):
                attempt = dict(trial)
                try:
                    make(attempt, item, count, [])
                except Short as short:
                    missing = str(short.args[0]) if short.args else ""
                    if missing in GATHERED or missing in MINED or missing in QUARRIED:
                        raw[missing] = raw.get(missing, 0) + 1
                        trial[missing] = trial.get(missing, 0) + 1
                        continue
                    break  # it waits for something no gathering brings
                attempt[item] = attempt.get(item, 0) - count  # spoken for
                trial = attempt
                break
        return raw
    return s.sensed("making raw needs", look)


def ingredients(items) -> set[str]:
    """Every item the recipes and smelting for `items` use, however deep, and the items themselves."""
    seen: set[str] = set()
    todo = list(items)
    while todo:
        item = todo.pop()
        if item in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(item)
        recipe = RECIPES.get(item)
        if recipe is not None:
            todo += list(recipe["ingredients"])
        if item in SMELTED:
            todo.append(SMELTED[item])
    return seen


def kept_for_making(s: Situation, item: str) -> float:
    """storage.KEEPS_MORE: a stack more of anything a project wants or makes what it wants from."""
    wanted = needs(s)
    if not wanted:
        return 0.0
    chain = s.sensed("making chain", lambda: ingredients(wanted))
    return float(STACK) if item in chain else 0.0


def ores_for_making(s: Situation) -> list[str]:
    """work.MORE_ORES: the ores raw_needs asks for."""
    return [ore for ore in MINED if raw_needs(s).get(ore, 0) > 0]


storage.KEEPS_MORE.append(kept_for_making)
work.MORE_ORES.append(ores_for_making)


# Making things -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------

def stations_for(steps: list[dict], near: set[str], inventory: dict[str, int]) -> list[str]:
    """The stations `steps` need that are not placed within reach (`near`): the crafting table first,
    since it makes the others (and is wanted for that when a furnace or kiln has to be made)."""
    wanted: list[str] = []
    for step in steps:
        if step["kind"] == "craft":
            station = RECIPES[step["recipe"]].get("station")
        elif step["kind"] == "smelt" and step["item"] not in COOKING:
            station = "kiln" if step["item"] in KILN_FIRED and "kiln" in near else "furnace"
        else:
            station = None
        if station is not None and station not in near and station not in wanted:
            wanted.append(station)
    making_one = any(inventory.get(station, 0) < 1 for station in wanted)
    if making_one and "crafting_table" not in near and "crafting_table" not in wanted:
        wanted.append("crafting_table")
    return sorted(wanted, key=lambda station: station != "crafting_table")


def craft_plan(s: Situation, items: dict[str, int]) -> list[dict] | None:
    """The steps that make `items` (as many of each as asked, what Mimo carries counting) with the
    stations they need; [] when Mimo carries them all already; None when they cannot all be made now
    or something made would not fit in Mimo's arms."""
    def made(inventory: dict[str, int], steps: list[dict]) -> None:
        for item, count in sorted(items.items()):
            make(inventory, item, count, steps)
            inventory[item] = inventory.get(item, 0) - count  # spoken for

    try:
        dry: list[dict] = []
        made(dict(s.inventory), dry)
        if not dry:
            return []
        x, _, z = s.here
        near = s.grid.placed_near(x, z, STATION_REACH, WORKSTATIONS)
        inventory, steps, placed, spots = dict(s.inventory), [], [], station_spots(s)
        for station in stations_for(dry, near, inventory):
            if inventory.get(station, 0) < 1:
                make(inventory, station, 1, steps)
            cell = place_station(spots, station, steps)
            if cell is None:
                raise Short(station)
            inventory[station] -= 1
            placed.append(cell)
        made(inventory, steps)
    except Short:
        return None
    steps.extend({"kind": "mine", "target": list(cell), "keep": True} for cell in reversed(placed))
    return steps if crafts_fit(s.inventory, steps) else None


def place_steps(s: Situation, stands, jobs: list[tuple[Cell, list[dict]]], at: Cell | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    """Each (cell, steps) job in turn, walking first to the stand nearest where Mimo will be (`at`,
    where it stands unless given) that reaches the cell, whenever the cell is out of reach from there
    or is where Mimo stands. A job no stand reaches is left out."""
    steps: list[dict] = []
    at = at or s.here
    for cell, work_steps in jobs:
        if math.dist(at, cell) > REACH or tuple(at) == tuple(cell):
            reaching = [stand for stand in stands if math.dist(stand, cell) <= REACH and tuple(stand) != tuple(cell)]
            if not reaching:
                continue
            at = min(reaching, key=lambda stand: (math.dist(stand, at), tuple(stand)))
            steps.append(whole_walk(at))
        steps.extend(work_steps)
    return steps


# gather_materials --------------------------------------------------------------------------------

def gathered_wanted(s: Situation) -> dict[str, int]:
    return {item: count for item, count in raw_needs(s).items() if item in GATHERED}


def diggable(s: Situation, cell: Cell, kind: str) -> bool:
    """Still there, with open air over ground (not water), nobody's that Mimo built or tends, and no
    failed step nearby."""
    x, y, z = cell
    if s.grid.material(*cell) != kind or reserved(s.grid, cell) or near_failure(s.state, cell):
        return False
    return kind in PLANTS or (s.grid.passable((x, y + 1, z)) and s.grid.material(x, y + 1, z) != "water")


def sources(s: Situation) -> list[tuple[Cell, str]]:
    """(cell, material) Mimo can gather for raw_needs within SOURCE_SIGHT blocks, nearest first, a
    stalk of sugar cane from its top down."""
    def look() -> list[tuple[Cell, str]]:
        wanted = gathered_wanted(s)
        x, _, z = s.here
        found: list[tuple[Cell, str]] = []
        plants = tuple(kind for kind in PLANTS if kind in wanted)
        if plants:
            for root in natural_plants(s.seed, x, z, SOURCE_SIGHT, plants):
                kind = s.grid.material(*root)
                top = root[1]
                while kind in plants and s.grid.material(root[0], top + 1, root[2]) == kind:
                    top += 1
                found += [((root[0], y, root[2]), kind) for y in range(root[1], top + 1)]
        ground = tuple(kind for kind in GROUND if kind in wanted)
        if ground:
            for gx in range(x - SOURCE_SIGHT, x + SOURCE_SIGHT + 1):
                for gz in range(z - SOURCE_SIGHT, z + SOURCE_SIGHT + 1):
                    if math.hypot(gx - x, gz - z) > SOURCE_SIGHT:
                        continue
                    kind = surface_material(gx, gz, s.seed)
                    if kind in ground:  # a lake bed's is under water: diggable leaves it
                        found.append(((gx, terrain_height(gx, gz, s.seed), gz), kind))
        found = [(cell, kind) for cell, kind in found if diggable(s, cell, kind)]
        return sorted(found, key=lambda entry: (math.hypot(entry[0][0] - x, entry[0][2] - z), -entry[0][1], entry[0]))
    return s.sensed("making sources", look)


def gather_valid(s: Situation) -> bool:
    return not s.night and bool(sources(s))


def gather_facts(s: Situation) -> str:
    wanted = ", ".join(f"{count} {item.replace('_', ' ')}" for item, count in sorted(gathered_wanted(s).items()))
    return f"wants {wanted} for what it makes; {len(sources(s))} to gather within {SOURCE_SIGHT} blocks"


def plan_gather(s: Situation, context: ActionContext) -> list[dict]:
    if s.night or s.brain["batches"] >= GATHER_BATCHES:
        return []
    wanted, jobs = dict(gathered_wanted(s)), []
    for cell, kind in sources(s):
        if wanted.get(kind, 0) > 0 and len(jobs) < PER_BATCH:
            wanted[kind] -= 1
            jobs.append((cell, [{"kind": "mine", "target": list(cell)}]))
    return reach_steps(s, jobs)


register(Purpose(
    "gather_materials", "gather materials",
    "Pick sugar cane and flowers, or dig clay and sand, for something it is making.",
    valid=gather_valid, facts=gather_facts,
    score=lambda s: max(0.0, 45.0 + s.trait("diligence") / 10 - late_penalty(s)), plan=plan_gather,
    thoughts=("I need a few more things for what I'm making.", "Clay by the water, sand, sugar cane...")))


def stone_for_making(s: Situation) -> float:
    """work.MORE_STONE: the cobblestone raw_needs asks for, a stack at most."""
    return float(min(STACK, raw_needs(s).get("cobblestone", 0)))


work.MORE_STONE.append(stone_for_making)
```

with:

```python
# Making: functions of the Situation giving more cobblestone gather_stone digs for (what making needs).
MORE_STONE: list = []


def stone_goal(s: Situation) -> float:
    """12 cobblestone, and the shelter's missing blocks on top; Making: and what MORE_STONE add (one that
    crashes adds nothing, logged once)."""
    more = 0.0
    for extra in MORE_STONE:
        try:
            more += float(extra(s))
        except Exception as error:
            log_once(logger, "more stone", error)
    return STONE_GOAL + building_need(s) + more
```

```python
def more_ores(s: Situation) -> list[str]:

    found: list[str] = []

        try:
            found += [ore for ore in wants(s) if ore not in found]
        except Exception as error:
            log_once(logger, "more ores", error)
```

### Task 4: The workshop

The workshop is a first shelter fitted out for making: a slab roof with a hatch, a crafting table, a furnace, a kiln and a barrel inside, bars in its windows, a seat and a sign (resolution 6). `build_workshop` raises it and fits it out, and the goal "A workshop" follows a home and iron tools. The test file's `Yard` (flat meadow, a finished home, a helper that carries out a plan's steps) is used again by Tasks 5 and 8–13.

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/survival/workshop.py`
- Modify: `backend/survival/blueprints.py`, `backend/survival/brain.py`, `backend/survival/building.py`, `backend/survival/making.py`, `backend/survival/structures.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_survival_workshop.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `blueprints`: `Blueprint`, `Planned`, `bill`, `find_site`, `shelter`, `style_for`; `building`: `MORE_BLOCKS`, `START_SHARE`, `carried_blocks`, `site_center`, `structural_batch`; `goals`: `Goal`, `Milestone`, `active`, `register_goal`; `home`: `all_structures`, `home_structure`; `making`: `NEEDS`, `after_steps`, `craft_plan`, `place_steps`; `pens`: `near_home`; `purposes`: `Purpose`, `register`; `situation`: `Situation`; `structures`: `blocked`, `blueprint_of`, `clearing`, `start`, `todo`; `foraging`: `reach_steps`, `whole_walk`.
- Produces:
  - `building`: `FINISHED_WORDS`, `MORE_BLOCKS`
  - `making`: `after_steps(inventory, steps) -> dict[str, int]`
  - `workshop`: `GOAL`, `KIND`, `WORKSHOP_REACH`, `SIZE`, `FIXTURES_PER_BATCH`, `WORKSHOP_BATCHES`, `ROOF`, `FITTED`, `FIXTURE_ORDER`, `FIXTURE`, `fitted_out(base, site, owner) -> Blueprint`, `design_workshop(grid, seed, center, traits, owner, salt) -> Blueprint | None`, `current_workshop(s) -> dict | None`, `workshop_design(s) -> Blueprint | None`, `fixtures_left(s, blueprint) -> list[Planned]`, `working_on(s) -> bool`, `workshop_needs(s) -> dict[str, int]`, `walls_left(s) -> int`, `slabs_first(s, blueprint) -> tuple[list[dict], Situation]`, `fixture_batch(s, blueprint) -> list[dict]`, `next_batch(s, blueprint) -> list[dict]`, `workshop_valid(s) -> bool`, `workshop_facts(s) -> str`, `plan_workshop(s, context) -> list[dict]`, `whole(done) -> float`, `workshop_blueprint(s) -> Blueprint | None`, `bricks_fired(s) -> float`, `raised(s) -> float`, `fixtures_in(s, names) -> float`
